That is ordinarily trivial, but some daemons\n112\t# hand the build a rootfs whose ROOT DIRECTORY is 0750:\n113\t#\n114\t#     drwxr-x---  1 root root  /\n115\t#\n116\t# `/` is then untraversable for every non-root uid, so such a process dies before\n117\t# it reaches main(), with the loader unable to resolve libc:\n118\t#\n119\t#     /bin/sh: error while loading shared libraries: libc.so.6:\n120\t#              cannot open shared object file: Permission denied\n121\t#\n122\t# `chmod a+rx /` repairs it — but a mode change on the rootfs ROOT DIRECTORY is\n123\t# not captured in a layer diff, so it is reverted at the start of the next RUN.\n124\t# (Verified: `RUN chmod 0750 / && ls -ld /` followed by `RUN ls -ld /` prints\n125\t# 0750 and then 0755.)  Two consequences, both load-bearing:\n126\t#\n127\t#   * the repair must happen in the SAME layer as the non-root process, so a bare\n128\t#     `USER postgres` stanza cannot work here no matter what precedes it;\n129\t#   * the shipped image keeps whatever `/` the daemon gives it, so the runtime\n130\t#     starter has to repeat the repair before it drops privileges.\n131\t#\n132\t# Hence this one helper, used by both the build steps below and by\n133\t# harbor_pg_start at verify time.  It is a no-op on a normal daemon.\n134\tRUN printf \"%s\\n\" \\\n135\t    \"#!/bin/sh\" \\\n136\t    \"# Run \\\"\\$@\\\" as the postgres user.  Repairs an untraversable / first: see\" \\\n137\t    \"# the long comment in the Dockerfile for why this cannot be a USER stanza.\" \\\n138\t    \"set -eu\" \\\n139\t    \"chmod a+rx /\" \\\n140\t    \"exec setpriv --reuid=postgres --regid=postgres --clear-groups \\\"\\$@\\\"\" \\\n141\t    > /usr/local/bin/harbor_as_postgres && chmod +x /usr/local/bin/harbor_as_postgres\n142\t\n143\t# Preflight: prove non-root execution works through the same path the cluster\n144\t# steps use, and say so plainly BEFORE initdb muddies the log.  A failure here\n145\t# with `/` already world-traversable in the listing means the restriction is\n146\t# imposed by the daemon or an LSM rather than by file modes — that is not fixable\n147\t# from this Dockerfile and belongs with whoever maintains the sandbox.\n148\tRUN echo \"== non-root preflight ==\" \\\n149\t && id postgres \\\n150\t && ls -ld / /usr/lib /usr/share /var/lib/postgresql \\\n151\t && if harbor_as_postgres /bin/sh -c 'echo NONROOT_OK'; then \\\n152\t      echo \"PREFLIGHT: non-root execution works\"; \\\n153\t    else \\\n154\t      echo \"NONROOT_STILL_BROKEN: cannot execute as postgres on this daemon.\" >&2; \\\n155\t      echo \"  PostgreSQL refuses to run as uid 0, so this image cannot build here.\" >&2; \\\n156\t      echo \"  Report the modes printed above to the sandbox maintainers.\" >&2; \\\n157\t      exit 1; \\\n158\t    fi\n159\t\n160\tRUN harbor_as_postgres \"$(ls -d /usr/lib/postgresql/*/bin)/initdb\" \\\n161\t      -D \"$PGDATA\" -A trust -U postgres --encoding=UTF8 --locale=C\n162\t\n163\t# Server tuning lives in postgresql.conf rather than in `pg_ctl -o`, so that the\n164\t# start command needs no nested quoting anywhere.  fsync/full_page_writes off is\n165\t# safe and much faster: the cluster is throwaway, recreated with every container.\n166\t#\n167\t# The extensions are installed into template1 as well as into the application\n168\t# database: pytest-django creates `test_pretix` from template1, and eventyay's\n169\t# test settings DISABLE MIGRATIONS (tests/testutils/settings.py) so the schema is\n170\t# built with `--run-syncdb` and the CreateExtension migrations never execute.\n171\t# Without pg_trgm the whole tickets suite dies on\n172\t# `operator class \"gin_trgm_ops\" does not exist`.\n173\tRUN printf \"%s\\n\" \\\n174\t    \"listen_addresses = '127.0.0.1'\" \\\n175\t    \"port = 5432\" \\\n176\t    \"fsync = off\" \\\n177\t    \"full_page_writes = off\" \\\n178\t    \"synchronous_commit = off\" \\\n179\t    \"max_connections = 100\" \\\n180\t    >> \"$PGDATA/postgresql.conf\"\n181\tRUN PGBIN=\"$(ls -d /usr/lib/postgresql/*/bin)\" \\\n182\t && harbor_as_postgres \"$PGBIN/pg_ctl\" -D \"$PGDATA\" -l /logs/postgres.log -w start \\\n183\t && harbor_as_postgres psql -h 127.0.0.1 -U postgres -c \"CREATE DATABASE pretix\" \\\n184\t && for _ext in pg_trgm btree_gin btree_gist unaccent citext hstore pgcrypto; do \\\n185\t      harbor_as_postgres psql -q -h 127.0.0.1 -U postgres -d template1 -c \"CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS $_ext\"; \\\n186\t      harbor_as_postgres psql -q -h 127.0.0.1 -U postgres -d pretix    -c \"CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS $_ext\"; \\\n187\t    done \\\n188\t && harbor_as_postgres \"$PGBIN/pg_ctl\" -D \"$PGDATA\" -w stop\n189\t\n190\t# One-line starter that every eventyay test.sh calls before pytest.\n191\t# Idempotent, offline, and waits until the server actually accepts connections.\n192\t# `\\$` keeps the expansion for RUN TIME instead of build time.\n193\tRUN printf \"%s\\n\" \\\n194\t    \"#!/bin/sh\" \\\n195\t    \"# Start the in-image PostgreSQL cluster (offline; loopback only). Idempotent.\" \\\n196\t    \"set -u\" \\\n197\t    \"PGBIN=\\$(ls -d /usr/lib/postgresql/*/bin)\" \\\n198\t    \"mkdir -p /var/run/postgresql /logs\" \\\n199\t    \"chown postgres:postgres /var/run/postgresql\" \\\n200\t    \"# The server must run as user postgres: PostgreSQL aborts on euid 0, and\" \\\n201\t    \"# harbor_as_postgres repairs an untraversable / on the way.  Fail loudly if\" \\\n202\t    \"# even that cannot execute, rather than after a 60-second pg_isready wait.\" \\\n203\t    \"harbor_as_postgres /bin/sh -c true || {\" \\\n204\t    \"  echo \\\"FATAL: cannot execute as postgres in this container; see \\\\\\`ls -ld /\\\\\\`.\\\" >&2\" \\\n205\t    \"  exit 1\" \\\n206\t    \"}\" \\\n207\t    \"harbor_as_postgres \\$PGBIN/pg_isready -q -h 127.0.0.1 -p 5432 && exit 0\" \\\n208\t    \"harbor_as_postgres \\$PGBIN/pg_ctl -D \\$PGDATA -l /logs/postgres.log -w start || true\" \\\n209\t    \"for _ in \\$(seq 1 60); do harbor_as_postgres \\$PGBIN/pg_isready -q -h 127.0.0.1 -p 5432 && exit 0; sleep 1; done\" \\\n210\t    \"echo \\\"postgres did not come up\\\" >&2\" \\\n211\t    \"exit 1\" \\\n212\t    > /usr/local/bin/harbor_pg_start && chmod +x /usr/local/bin/harbor_pg_start\n213\t\n214\t# --- harness plumbing ---------------------------------------------------------\n215\t# Visual evidence from the issue, readable by the agent at /app/.harbor_assets/.\n216\t# tests/ and solution/ are intentionally NOT baked in — the harness mounts them\n217\t# at verify time.\n218\tRUN mkdir -p /logs /app/.harbor_assets\n219\tCOPY assets/ /app/.harbor_assets/\n220\t\n221\tWORKDIR /app\n222\tCMD [\"bash\"]\n223\t"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"b67d529b-0651-43d8-89d4-007d7c025402","uuid":"68d48d7f-f9e3-44f8-8b38-8f292a7058e7","timestamp":"2026-08-15T20:48:36.941Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/app/task/environment/Dockerfile","content":"# =============================================================================\n# fossasia/eventyay — Harbor task image (DONOR RECIPE)\n#\n# eventyay is a single Django 6 monolith (a merge of pretix + pretalx + video)\n# living in the repo sub-directory `app/`. That is ordinarily trivial, but some daemons\n# hand the build a rootfs whose ROOT DIRECTORY is 0750:\n#\n#     drwxr-x---  1 root root  /\n#\n# `/` is then untraversable for every non-root uid, so such a process dies before\n# it reaches main(), with the loader unable to resolve libc:\n#\n#     /bin/sh: error while loading shared libraries: libc.so.6:\n#              cannot open shared object file: Permission denied\n#\n# `chmod a+rx /` repairs it — but a mode change on the rootfs ROOT DIRECTORY is\n# not captured in a layer diff, so it is reverted at the start of the next RUN.\n# (Verified: `RUN chmod 0750 / && ls -ld /` followed by `RUN ls -ld /` prints\n# 0750 and then 0755.)  Two consequences, both load-bearing:\n#\n#   * the repair must happen in the SAME layer as the non-root process, so a bare\n#     `USER postgres` stanza cannot work here no matter what precedes it;\n#   * the shipped image keeps whatever `/` the daemon gives it, so the runtime\n#     starter has to repeat the repair before it drops privileges.\n#\n# Hence this one helper, used by both the build steps below and by\n# harbor_pg_start at verify time.  It is a no-op on a normal daemon.\nRUN printf \"%s\\n\" \\\n    \"#!/bin/sh\" \\\n    \"# Run \\\"\\$@\\\" as the postgres user.  Repairs an untraversable / first: see\" \\\n    \"# the long comment in the Dockerfile for why this cannot be a USER stanza.\" \\\n    \"set -eu\" \\\n    \"chmod a+rx /\" \\\n    \"exec setpriv --reuid=postgres --regid=postgres --clear-groups \\\"\\$@\\\"\" \\\n    > /usr/local/bin/harbor_as_postgres && chmod +x /usr/local/bin/harbor_as_postgres\n\n# Preflight: prove non-root execution works through the same path the cluster\n# steps use, and say so plainly BEFORE initdb muddies the log.  A failure here\n# with `/` already world-traversable in the listing means the restriction is\n# imposed by the daemon or an LSM rather than by file modes — that is not fixable\n# from this Dockerfile and belongs with whoever maintains the sandbox.\nRUN echo \"== non-root preflight ==\" \\\n && id postgres \\\n && ls -ld / /usr/lib /usr/share /var/lib/postgresql \\\n && if harbor_as_postgres /bin/sh -c 'echo NONROOT_OK'; then \\\n      echo \"PREFLIGHT: non-root execution works\"; \\\n    else \\\n      echo \"NONROOT_STILL_BROKEN: cannot execute as postgres on this daemon.\" >&2; \\\n      echo \"  PostgreSQL refuses to run as uid 0, so this image cannot build here.\" >&2; \\\n      echo \"  Report the modes printed above to the sandbox maintainers.\" >&2; \\\n      exit 1; \\\n    fi\n\nRUN harbor_as_postgres \"$(ls -d /usr/lib/postgresql/*/bin)/initdb\" \\\n      -D \"$PGDATA\" -A trust -U postgres --encoding=UTF8 --locale=C\n\n# Server tuning lives in postgresql.conf rather than in `pg_ctl -o`, so that the\n# start command needs no nested quoting anywhere.  fsync/full_page_writes off is\n# safe and much faster: the cluster is throwaway, recreated with every container.\n#\n# The extensions are installed into template1 as well as into the application\n# database: pytest-django creates `test_pretix` from template1, and eventyay's\n# test settings DISABLE MIGRATIONS (tests/testutils/settings.py) so the schema is\n# built with `--run-syncdb` and the CreateExtension migrations never execute.\n# Without pg_trgm the whole tickets suite dies on\n# `operator class \"gin_trgm_ops\" does not exist`.\nRUN printf \"%s\\n\" \\\n    \"listen_addresses = '127.0.0.1'\" \\\n    \"port = 5432\" \\\n    \"fsync = off\" \\\n    \"full_page_writes = off\" \\\n    \"synchronous_commit = off\" \\\n    \"max_connections = 100\" \\\n    >> \"$PGDATA/postgresql.conf\"\nRUN PGBIN=\"$(ls -d /usr/lib/postgresql/*/bin)\" \\\n && harbor_as_postgres \"$PGBIN/pg_ctl\" -D \"$PGDATA\" -l /logs/postgres.log -w start \\\n && harbor_as_postgres psql -h 127.0.0.1 -U postgres -c \"CREATE DATABASE pretix\" \\\n && for _ext in pg_trgm btree_gin btree_gist unaccent citext hstore pgcrypto; do \\\n      harbor_as_postgres psql -q -h 127.0.0.1 -U postgres -d template1 -c \"CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS $_ext\"; \\\n      harbor_as_postgres psql -q -h 127.0.0.1 -U postgres -d pretix    -c \"CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS $_ext\"; \\\n    done \\\n && harbor_as_postgres \"$PGBIN/pg_ctl\" -D \"$PGDATA\" -w stop\n\n# One-line starter that every eventyay test.sh calls before pytest.\n# Idempotent, offline, and waits until the server actually accepts connections.\n# `\\$` keeps the expansion for RUN TIME instead of build time.\nRUN printf \"%s\\n\" \\\n    \"#!/bin/sh\" \\\n    \"# Start the in-image PostgreSQL cluster (offline; loopback only). Idempotent.\" \\\n    \"set -u\" \\\n    \"PGBIN=\\$(ls -d /usr/lib/postgresql/*/bin)\" \\\n    \"mkdir -p /var/run/postgresql /logs\" \\\n    \"chown postgres:postgres /var/run/postgresql\" \\\n    \"# The server must run as user postgres: PostgreSQL aborts on euid 0, and\" \\\n    \"# harbor_as_postgres repairs an untraversable / on the way.  Fail loudly if\" \\\n    \"# even that cannot execute, rather than after a 60-second pg_isready wait.\" \\\n    \"harbor_as_postgres /bin/sh -c true || {\" \\\n    \"  echo \\\"FATAL: cannot execute as postgres in this container; see \\\\\\`ls -ld /\\\\\\`.\\\" >&2\" \\\n    \"  exit 1\" \\\n    \"}\" \\\n    \"harbor_as_postgres \\$PGBIN/pg_isready -q -h 127.0.0.1 -p 5432 && exit 0\" \\\n    \"harbor_as_postgres \\$PGBIN/pg_ctl -D \\$PGDATA -l /logs/postgres.log -w start || true\" \\\n    \"for _ in \\$(seq 1 60); do harbor_as_postgres \\$PGBIN/pg_isready -q -h 127.0.0.1 -p 5432 && exit 0; sleep 1; done\" \\\n    \"echo \\\"postgres did not come up\\\" >&2\" \\\n    \"exit 1\" \\\n    > /usr/local/bin/harbor_pg_start && chmod +x /usr/local/bin/harbor_pg_start\n\n# --- harness plumbing ---------------------------------------------------------\n# Visual evidence from the issue, readable by the agent at /app/.harbor_assets/.\n# tests/ and solution/ are intentionally NOT baked in — the harness mounts them\n# at verify time.\nRUN mkdir -p /logs /app/.harbor_assets\nCOPY assets/ /app/.harbor_assets/\n\nWORKDIR /app\nCMD [\"bash\"]\n","numLines":223,"startLine":1,"totalLines":223}}}

_assert_artworks_do_not_overlap(env):\n394\t+    sheet = Sheet(_export(env['event'], env['products'], env['four_up'])[2])\n395\t+    n = len(env['products'])\n396\t+    per_page = []\n397\t+    for region = _region(page, rgb)\n402\t+            if region is not None and region['count'] >= 0.05 * page_px:\n403\t+                present.append((i, region))\n404\t+        per_page.append(present)\n405\t+        for a in range(len(present)):\n406\t+            for b in range(a + 1, len(present)):\n407\t+                box_a, box_b = _box(present[a][1]), badge per sheet, or several\n+badges tiled onto a single sheet.  Whatever option is chosen, the printed result\n+must be the same badge -- its artwork and its printed text together -- simply\n+placed into its slot on the sheet.\n+\n+Everything asserted here is measured from the *rendered pixels* of the produced\n+PDF (poppler raster) and every expectation is recomputed at run time from the\n+fixture itself: from the artwork colours that were uploaded, from the sheet size\n+that came back, and from what the very same badge looks like when it is printed\n+one-per-sheet.  No symbol, key, helper name, module name or magic constant of\n+any implementation appears in an assertion.\n+\n+Two independent, fully disjoint fixture palettes are used (\"harbourfog\" and\n+\"nordkap\"): different artwork colours, different text colours, different badge\n+orientation, different badge counts and different rendering options.\n+\"\"\"\n+\n+import glob\n+import json\n+import math\n+import os\n+import re\n+import subprocess\n+import tempfile\n+from datetime import timedelta\n+from decimal import Decimal\n+from io import BytesIO\n+\n+import numpy as np\n+import pytest\n+from django.core.files.base import ContentFile\n+from django.utils.timezone import now\n+from django_scopes import scopes_disabled\n+from PIL import Image\n+from pypdf import PdfReader, PdfWriter\n+from reportlab.lib.units import mm\n+from reportlab.pdfgen import canvas as rl_canvas\n+\n+from eventyay.base.models import Event, Order, OrderPosition, Organizer, Product\n+from eventyay.base.services.orders import OrderError\n+from eventyay.plugins.badges.exporters import BadgeExporter\n+from eventyay.plugins.badges.models import BadgeProduct\n+\n+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n+# Paper geometry.  These are the ISO paper sizes in PostScript points, i.e. a\n+# property of the paper, not of the application.\n+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n+A4_SHORT = 595.2755905511812\n+A4_LONG = 841.8897637795277\n+A4_PORTRAIT = (A4_SHORT, A4_LONG)\n+A4_LANDSCAPE = (A4_LONG, A4_SHORT)\n+# Exactly half an A4 sheet in each direction -- \"A6\" as the rendering options use it.\n+HALF_A4_PORTRAIT = (A4_SHORT / 2, A4_LONG / 2)\n+HALF_A4_LANDSCAPE = (A4_LONG / 2, A4_SHORT / 2)\n+\n+DPI = 150\n+PT_PER_INCH = 72.0\n+COLOUR_TOL = 25  # L1 distance; solid fills come back exact, this only absorbs rounding\n+MIN_MARK_PX = 1200  # a printed text mark is ~15k px at full size, ~3.8k at half size\n+ART_MIN_FRAC = 0.02  # an artwork really printed on a sheet covers far more than 2% of it\n+\n+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n+# Palette \"harbourfog\": four landscape badges, four-up on a landscape sheet.\n+# Palette \"nordkap\": six portrait badges, four-up and eight-up on A4 sheets.\n+# The two colour sets are disjoint; inside a set every colour is at least\n+# L1-distance 100 from every other one, from white, and from every blend of any\n+# two painted colours -- so an anti-aliased edge can never be misread as a\n+# different badge's colour.\n+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n+HARBOURFOG_ART = [(230, 0, 0), (0, 230, 0), (0, 0, 230), (230, 230, 0)]\n+HARBOURFOG_INK = [(120, 0, 0), (0, 120, 0), (0, 0, 120), (120, 60, 180)]\n+\n+NORDKAP_ART = [\n+    (200, 40, 140),\n+    (40, 200, 140),\n+    (140, 40, 200),\n+    (200, 140, 40),\n+    (40, 140, 200),\n+    (140, 200, 40),\n+]\n+NORDKAP_INK = [\n+    (100, 0, 50),\n+    (0, 100, 50),\n+    (50, 0, 100),\n+    (100, 50, 0),\n+    (0, 50, 100),\n+    (50, 100, 0),\n+]\n+\n+# Names taken verbatim from the vocabulary a fix is likely to introduce, planted\n+# in the fixture so that they already exist on an unfixed tree.  A suite that had\n+# accidentally been keyed to such names instead of to the rendered result would\n+# pass before the fix and be caught here.\n+CANARY_WORDS = [\n+    'render_nup',\n+    'merge_foreground_buffer',\n+    'correct_page_media_box',\n+    'badge_default_a6l',\n+    'ReportLab Generated PDF',\n+    'empty.pdf',\n+]\n+\n+\n+@pytest.fixture(autouse=True)\n+def _no_scopes():\n+    with scopes_disabled():\n+        yield\n+\n+\n+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n+# Fixture artwork\n+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n+def _artwork_pdf(rgb, size_pt):\n+    \"\"\"A single-page badge artwork: a solid colour field with a white text panel.\n+\n+    The file is deliberately a substantial, fully formed document (tens of kB of\n+    metadata) so that it is plainly real artwork and not a stand-in.\n+    \"\"\"\n+    width, height = size_pt\n+    buf = BytesIO()\n+    c = rl_canvas.Canvas(buf, pagesize=(width, height))\n+    c.setFillColorRGB(rgb[0] / 255.0, rgb[1] / 255.0, rgb[2] / 255.0)\n+    c.rect(0, 0, width, height, stroke=0, fill=1)\n+    panel_w = min(width * 0.86, 230.0)\n+    panel_h = 92.0\n+    c.setFillColorRGB(1, 1, 1)\n+    c.rect((width - panel_w) / 2, height / 2 - panel_h / 2, panel_w, panel_h, stroke=0, fill=1)\n+    c.showPage()\n+    c.save()\n+    buf.seek(0)\n+    reader = PdfReader(buf)\n+    writer = PdfWriter()\n+    writer.add_page(reader.pages[0])\n+    writer.add_metadata({'/Title': 'Badge artwork', '/Subject': 'artwork ' * 6000})\n+    out = BytesIO()\n+    writer.write(out)\n+    return out.getvalue()\n+\n+\n+def _layout_json(ink, art, size_pt, canary):\n+    \"\"\"A badge layout with one big text mark centred on the artwork's white panel.\"\"\"\n+    width, height = size_pt\n+    width_mm = width / mm\n+    height_mm = height / mm\n+    return json.dumps(\n+        [\n+            {\n+                'type': 'textarea',\n+                'left': '4',\n+                'bottom': '%.3f' % (height_mm / 2 - 6.0),\n+                'fontsize': '54',\n+                'color': [ink[0], ink[1], ink[2], 1],\n+                'fontfamily': 'Open Sans',\n+                'bold': True,\n+                'italic': False,\n+                'width': '%.3f' % (width_mm - 8),\n+                'content': 'other',\n+                'text': 'HHH',\n+                'align': 'center',\n+            },\n+            {\n+                # deliberately invisible: same colour as the artwork behind it.\n+                'type': 'textarea',\n+                'left': '1',\n+                'bottom': '1',\n+                'fontsize': '3',\n+                'color': [art[0], art[1], art[2], 1],\n+                'fontfamily': 'Open Sans',\n+                'bold': False,\n+                'italic': False,\n+                'width': '%.3f' % (width_mm - 2),\n+                'content': 'other',\n+                'text': canary,\n+                'align': 'left',\n+            },\n+        ]\n+    )\n+\n+\n+class Sheet:\n+    \"\"\"A rendered export, as pixels.\"\"\"\n+\n+    def __init__(self, pdf_bytes):\n+        self.pdf_bytes = pdf_bytes\n+        reader = PdfReader(BytesIO(pdf_bytes))\n+        self.page_sizes = [\n+            (float(p.mediabox.width), float(p.mediabox.height)) for p in reader.pages\n+        ]\n+        self.pages = _rasterise(pdf_bytes)\n+        assert len(self.pages) == len(self.page_sizes)\n+\n+    def __len__(self):\n+        return len(self.pages)\n+\n+\n+def _rasterise(pdf_bytes):\n+    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as d:\n+        src = os.path.join(d, 'sheet.pdf')\n+        with open(src, 'wb') as fh:\n+            fh.write(pdf_bytes)\n+        subprocess.run(\n+            ['pdftoppm', '-r', str(DPI), '-png', src, os.path.join(d, 'page')],\n+            check=True,\n+            capture_output=True,\n+        )\n+        files = glob.glob(os.path.join(d, 'page-*.png'))\n+        files.sort(key=lambda p: int(re.search(r'page-(\\d+)\\.png$', p).group(1)))\n+        return [np.asarray(Image.open(p).convert('RGB'), dtype=np.int16) for p in files]\n+\n+\n+def _region(page, rgb):\n+    \"\"\"Where a given ink/artwork colour landed on one rendered page.\"\"\"\n+    dist = np.abs(page - np.array(rgb, dtype=np.int16)).sum(axis=2)\n+    mask = dist <= COLOUR_TOL\n+    count = int(mask.sum())\n+    if count == 0:\n+        return None\n+    ys, xs = np.nonzero(mask)\n+    return {\n+        'count': count,\n+        'x0': int(xs.min()),\n+        'y0': int(ys.min()),\n+        'x1': int(xs.max()) + 1,\n+        'y1': int(ys.max()) + 1,\n+    }\n+\n+\n+def _box(region):\n+    return (region['x0'], region['y0'], region['x1'], region['y1'])\n+\n+\n+def _area(box):\n+    return max(0, box[2] - box[0]) * max(0, box[3] - box[1])\n+\n+\n+def _intersection(a, b):\n+    return (max(a[0], b[0]), max(a[1], b[1]), min(a[2], b[2]), min(a[3], b[3]))\n+\n+\n+def _centre(region):\n+    return ((region['x0'] + region['x1']) / 2.0, (region['y0'] + region['y1']) / 2.0)\n+\n+\n+def _find_all(sheet, colours, min_count=0, min_frac=0.0):\n+    \"\"\"colour index -> (page index, region), for every colour that really printed.\"\"\"\n+    found = {}\n+    for i, rgb in enumerate(colours):\n+        for page_no, page in enumerate(sheet.pages):\n+            page_px = page.shape[0] * page.shape[1]\n+            floor = max(min_count, min_frac * page_px)\n+            region = _region(page, rgb)\n+            if region is not None and region['count'] >= floor:\n+                assert i not in found, (\n+                    'artwork/ink colour %s printed on more than one sheet page' % (rgb,)\n+                )\n+                found[i] = (page_no, region)\n+    return found\n+\n+\n+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n+# Event fixtures\n+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n+def _build_event(slug, badge_size_pt, art_colours, ink_colours):\n+    organizer = Organizer.objects.create(name='Harbor %s' % slug, slug=slug)\n+    event = Event.objects.create(\n+        organizer=organizer,\n+        name='Badge print %s' % slug,\n+        slug=slug,\n+        date_from=now() + timedelta(days=90),\n+        live=True,\n+    )\n+    products = []\n+    for i, (art, ink) in enumerate(zip(art_colours, ink_colours)):\n+        canary = CANARY_WORDS[i % len(CANARY_WORDS)]\n+        product = Product.objects.create(\n+            event=event,\n+            name='%s ticket %d' % (canary, i),\n+            default_price=Decimal('10.00'),\n+            admission=True,\n+        )\n+        layout = event.badge_layouts.create(\n+            name='%s %d' % (canary, i),\n+            default=(i == 0),\n+            layout=_layout_json(ink, art, badge_size_pt, canary),\n+        )\n+        layout.background.save('artwork-%d.pdf' % i, ContentFile(_artwork_pdf(art, badge_size_pt)))\n+        BadgeProduct.objects.create(product=product, layout=layout)\n+        products.append(product)\n+\n+    order = Order.objects.create(\n+        code='NUPXY%d' % len(products),\n+        event=event,\n+        email='badges@example.org',\n+        status=Order.STATUS_PAID,\n+        datetime=now(),\n+        expires=now() + timedelta(days=10),\n+        total=Decimal('10.00') * len(products),\n+    )\n+    for i, product in enumerate(products):\n+        OrderPosition.objects.create(\n+            order=order,\n+            product=product,\n+            price=Decimal('10.00'),\n+            attendee_name_parts={'_legacy': '%s %d' % (CANARY_WORDS[i % len(CANARY_WORDS)], i)},\n+            secret='harborsecret%02d' % i,\n+            positionid=i + 1,\n+        )\n+    return event, products\n+\n+\n+def _export(event, products, rendering):\n+    fname, ftype, payload = BadgeExporter(event).render(\n+        {\n+            'products': [p.pk for p in products],\n+            'rendering': rendering,\n+            'include_pending': True,\n+        }\n+    )\n+    return fname, ftype, payload\n+\n+\n+@pytest.fixture\n+def harbourfog():\n+    \"\"\"Four landscape badges, printed four-up on a landscape sheet.\"\"\"\n+    event, products = _build_event(\n+        'harbourfog', HALF_A4_LANDSCAPE, HARBOURFOG_ART, HARBOURFOG_INK\n+    )\n+    return {\n+        'event': event,\n+        'products': products,\n+        'art': HARBOURFOG_ART,\n+        'ink': HARBOURFOG_INK,\n+        'badge_size': HALF_A4_LANDSCAPE,\n+        'four_up': 'a4_a6l',\n+        'four_up_sheet': A4_LANDSCAPE,\n+        'eight_up': 'a4_a7l',\n+        'eight_up_sheet': A4_PORTRAIT,\n+        'eight_up_grid': (2, 4),\n+    }\n+\n+\n+@pytest.fixture\n+def nordkap():\n+    \"\"\"Six portrait badges, printed four-up and eight-up on A4 sheets.\"\"\"\n+    event, products = _build_event('nordkap', HALF_A4_PORTRAIT, NORDKAP_ART, NORDKAP_INK)\n+    return {\n+        'event': event,\n+        'products': products,\n+        'art': NORDKAP_ART,\n+        'ink': NORDKAP_INK,\n+        'badge_size': HALF_A4_PORTRAIT,\n+        'four_up': 'a4_a6p',\n+        'four_up_sheet': A4_PORTRAIT,\n+        'eight_up': 'a4_a7p',\n+        'eight_up_sheet': A4_LANDSCAPE,\n+        'eight_up_grid': (4, 2),\n+    }\n+\n+\n+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n+# Shared assertions\n+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n+def _assert_every_artwork_printed(env):\n+    sheet = Sheet(_export(env['event'], env['products'], env['four_up'])[2])\n+    n = len(env['products'])\n+    slot_share = 1.0 / 4.0  # four badges to a sheet\n+    found = _find_all(sheet, env['art'], min_frac=ART_MIN_FRAC)\n+    missing = [i for i in range(n) if i not in found]\n+    assert not missing, (\n+        'the artwork of badge(s) %s never reached the printed sheet; only %d of %d '\n+        'badge artworks printed at all' % (missing, len(found), n)\n+    )\n+    for i, (page_no, region) in sorted(found.items()):\n+        page_px = sheet.pages[page_no].shape[0] * sheet.pages[page_no].shape[1]\n+        share = region['count'] / float(page_px)\n+        assert share > slot_share * 0.6, (\n+            'badge %d covers only %.1f%% of its sheet; a badge printed four to a '\n+            'sheet must fill about %.0f%% of it' % (i, 100 * share, 100 * slot_share)\n+        )\n+\n+\n+def _assert_artworks_do_not_overlap(env):\n+    sheet = Sheet(_export(env['event'], env['products'], env['four_up'])[2])\n+    n = len(env['products'])\n+    per_page = []\n+    for page_no, page in enumerate(sheet.pages):\n+        page_px = page.shape[0] * page.shape[1]\n+        present = []\n+        for i, rgb in enumerate(env['art']):\n+            region = _region(page, rgb)\n+            if region is not None and region['count'] >= 0.05 * page_px:\n+                present.append((i, region))\n+        per_page.append(present)\n+        for a in range(len(present)):\n+            for b in range(a + 1, len(present)):\n+                box_a, box_b = _box(present[a][1]), _box(present[b][1])\n+                inter = _area(_intersection(box_a, box_b))\n+                smaller = min(_area(box_a), _area(box_b))\n+                assert inter <= 0.03 * smaller, (\n+                    'badge %d and badge %d overlap on sheet page %d (%.0f%% of a badge)'\n+                    % (present[a][0], present[b][0], page_no, 100.0 * inter / smaller)\n+                )\n+\n+    # A four-up sheet may not carry more than four badge artworks on a page, and\n+    # every badge's artwork must reach the sheet somewhere. The exact split\n+    # across pages is deliberately NOT pinned: any tiling the export chooses is\n+    # accepted so long as no page is overfilled and no artwork is lost.\n+    counts = sorted(len(p) for p in per_page)\n+    overfull = [c for c in counts if c > 4]\n+    assert not overfull, (\n+        'a four-up sheet page carries %s badge artworks; no page may carry more '\n+        'than four (printed %s)' % (overfull, counts)\n+    )\n+    printed = sum(counts)\n+    assert printed == n, (\n+        'expected all %d badge artworks somewhere on the sheet, printed %d '\n+        '(per page: %s)' % (n, printed, counts)\n+    )\n+\n+\n+def _assert_mark_sits_on_its_own_artwork(env):\n+    sheet = Sheet(_export(env['event'], env['products'], env['four_up'])[2])\n+    art = _find_all(sheet, env['art'], min_frac=ART_MIN_FRAC)\n+    ink = _find_all(sheet, env['ink'], min_count=MIN_MARK_PX)\n+    for i in range(len(env['products'])):\n+        assert i in ink, 'the printed text of badge %d is missing from the sheet' % i\n+        assert i in art, 'the artwork of badge %d is missing from the sheet' % i\n+        assert ink[i][0] == art[i][0], (\n+            'badge %d printed its text on sheet page %d but its artwork on page %d'\n+            % (i, ink[i][0], art[i][0])\n+        )\n+        text_box = _box(ink[i][1])\n+        art_box = _box(art[i][1])\n+        grown = (art_box[0] - 3, art_box[1] - 3, art_box[2] + 3, art_box[3] + 3)\n+        assert _area(_intersection(text_box, grown)) >= 0.98 * _area(text_box), (\n+            'the text of badge %d (%s) is not printed on its own artwork (%s)'\n+            % (i, text_box, art_box)\n+        )\n+\n+\n+def _assert_sheet_badge_matches_lone_badge(env):\n+    \"\"\"A badge on a shared sheet must be the same badge it is on its own sheet.\"\"\"\n+    lone = Sheet(_export(env['event'], env['products'], 'one')[2])\n+    lone_art = _find_all(lone, env['art'], min_frac=ART_MIN_FRAC)\n+    lone_ink = _find_all(lone, env['ink'], min_count=MIN_MARK_PX)\n+    assert len(lone_art) == len(env['products'])\n+    assert len(lone_ink) == len(env['products'])\n+\n+    sheet = Sheet(_export(env['event'], env['products'], env['four_up'])[2])\n+    sheet_art = _find_all(sheet, env['art'], min_frac=ART_MIN_FRAC)\n+    sheet_ink = _find_all(sheet, env['ink'], min_count=MIN_MARK_PX)\n+\n+    for i in range(len(env['products'])):\n+        assert i in sheet_art, 'badge %d lost its artwork on the shared sheet' % i\n+        assert i in sheet_ink, 'badge %d lost its printed text on the shared sheet' % i\n+\n+        lp, lr = lone_art[i]\n+        lone_page = lone.pages[lp]\n+        lone_h, lone_w = lone_page.shape[0], lone_page.shape[1]\n+        lone_ink_box = _box(lone_ink[i][1])\n+        # where the text sits inside the badge, as a fraction of the badge\n+        want = (\n+            (lone_ink_box[0] + lone_ink_box[2]) / 2.0 / lone_w,\n+            (lone_ink_box[1] + lone_ink_box[3]) / 2.0 / lone_h,\n+        )\n+        lone_fill = lr['count'] / float(lone_w * lone_h)\n+\n+        sp, sr = sheet_art[i]\n+        slot = _box(sr)\n+        slot_w = float(slot[2] - slot[0])\n+        slot_h = float(slot[3] - slot[1])\n+        ink_box = _box(sheet_ink[i][1])\n+        got = (\n+            ((ink_box[0] + ink_box[2]) / 2.0 - slot[0]) / slot_w,\n+            ((ink_box[1] + ink_box[3]) / 2.0 - slot[1]) / slot_h,\n+        )\n+        assert abs(got[0] - want[0]) < 0.05 and abs(got[1] - want[1]) < 0.05, (\n+            'badge %d prints its text at %s of its slot on the shared sheet but at '\n+            '%s of the sheet when printed on its own' % (i, got, want)\n+        )\n+        sheet_fill = sr['count'] / (slot_w * slot_h)\n+        assert abs(sheet_fill - lone_fill) < 0.06, (\n+            'badge %d covers %.1f%% of its slot on the shared sheet but %.1f%% of '\n+            'its own sheet' % (i, 100 * sheet_fill, 100 * lone_fill)\n+        )\n+\n+\n+def _assert_layout_is_a_regular_grid(env, rendering, grid):\n+    \"\"\"Badges must be laid out on the grid the rendering option describes.\"\"\"\n+    cols, rows = grid\n+    sheet = Sheet(_export(env['event'], env['products'], rendering)[2])\n+    per_page = cols * rows\n+    n = len(env['products'])\n+\n+    marks = _find_all(sheet, env['ink'], min_count=MIN_MARK_PX)\n+    missing = [i for i in range(n) if i not in marks]\n+    assert not missing, (\n+        'badge(s) %s printed nothing readable on the %d-up sheet -- their content '\n+        'fell outside the printable area' % (missing, per_page)\n+    )\n+\n+    by_page = {}\n+    for i, (page_no, region) in marks.items():\n+        by_page.setdefault(page_no, []).append((i, _centre(region)))\n+\n+    for page_no, entries in by_page.items():\n+        page = sheet.pages[page_no]\n+        height_px, width_px = page.shape[0], page.shape[1]\n+        cell_w = width_px / float(cols)\n+        cell_h = height_px / float(rows)\n+        tol = 0.04 * min(cell_w, cell_h)\n+        ref = entries[0][1]\n+        for i, centre in entries[1:]:\n+            for axis, pitch, name in ((0, cell_w, 'across'), (1, cell_h, 'down')):\n+                delta = centre[axis] - ref[axis]\n+                steps = round(delta / pitch)\n+                assert abs(delta - steps * pitch) <= tol, (\n+                    'badge %d sits %.1f px %s from badge %d on sheet page %d, which '\n+                    'is not a whole number of %.1f px slots'\n+                    % (i, delta, name, entries[0][0], page_no, pitch)\n+                )\n+        occupied = set()\n+        for i, centre in entries:\n+            col = int(centre[0] // cell_w)\n+            row = int(centre[1] // cell_h)\n+            assert (row, col) not in occupied, (\n+                'two badges share slot %s on sheet page %d' % ((row, col), page_no)\n+            )\n+            occupied.add((row, col))\n+        assert len(entries) <= per_page\n+\n+\n+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n+# harbourfog\n+#
